##### Other / [Other](#other-1)

* [zanadir](https://mustachecase.github.io/) — Audits CI configuration (GitHub Actions, GitLab CI, CircleCI, Bitbucket Pipelines, Azure Pipelines, Jenkins) for missing security and quality controls such as SCA, secrets detection, SAST, IaC and workflow security, and grades whether each present control can actually fail the build (continue-on-error, || true, --exit-code 0, push-only triggers). Outputs table, JSON, SARIF or Markdown and prints CI snippets for the gaps.

#### [7. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

* [AutoHarness (⭐2)](https://github.com/D-Shey/autoharness) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-21 - Makes Gemini CLI (and Claude Code, Codex, Qwen Code) prepare before it builds: splits the task into areas of know-how, finds or writes a skill for each, checks the result against the original, and lets a fresh critic judge it. MIT.

##### MCP Servers

* [Firecrawl](https://docs.firecrawl.dev/quickstarts/gemini-cli?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=integration) - Official Firecrawl MCP server for web search, page scraping to markdown or JSON, and site crawling. The hosted server works keyless (rate limited) with search, scrape and parse; add an [API key](https://docs.firecrawl.dev/mcp-server/keyless?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=integration#add-an-api-key) for crawl, map and the rest. The open-source server is MIT ([source (⭐7.7k)](https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-mcp-server) ⭐ 7,667 | 🐛 180 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-22). Add with: `gemini mcp add --transport http firecrawl https://mcp.firecrawl.dev/v2/mcp`.
* [npmscan (⭐1)](https://github.com/salemalem/npmscan-mcp-plugin) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-21 - npm supply-chain security: checks packages for malware, known vulnerabilities, typosquats, maintainer takeovers and risky install scripts before install, and audits package.json, lockfiles, SBOMs and PR dependency changes. 23 read-only tools, no API key, no signup. Remote Streamable HTTP, bundled with 5 skills as a Gemini CLI extension. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/salemalem/npmscan-mcp-plugin`.
* [deed.page](https://deed.page/docs?ref=skill-awesome-gemini-cli) - Static hosting for agents with a remote MCP server that lets Gemini CLI publish an HTML file or static folder to a permanent public `https://<slug>.deed.page` URL and update it with the token it gets back. No account or API key for publishing; the companion Agent Skill is MIT ([source (⭐1)](https://github.com/f-o-x11/deed-page-skill) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-10-22). Remote Streamable HTTP. Add with `gemini mcp add --transport http deed-page https://deed.page/mcp`.

##### Documentation & Examples

* [MCP servers, harness by harness (RigFit)](https://rigfit-harness.vercel.app/mcp-servers-by-harness/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - One MCP server (Context7) configured in Gemini CLI's settings.json alongside Claude Code, Codex CLI, OpenCode and goose, using each tool's documented syntax, with sources dated.

* [Logly](https://logly.uk/) - Cookieless web analytics with a sub-1KB tracker. Bots are counted separately and labelled by type — search engines, AI crawlers, SEO tools, monitors — instead of being filtered out silently. `©` `SaaS`
* [PageLens](https://pagelens.io/) - Cookieless, EU-hosted web analytics with revenue by traffic source and AI Visibility: which pages ChatGPT, Claude and Perplexity read, use in answers and send visitors to. ([Demo](https://pagelens.io/demo)) `©` `SaaS` `EU`
* [Lomen](https://lomenanalytics.com/) - Cookie-free website analytics with an AI you can ask about your traffic, revenue from Stripe, Shopify, Lemon Squeezy and Polar next to visitors, and tracking of visits from ChatGPT, Claude and Perplexity. ([Demo](https://lomenanalytics.com/demo)) `©` `SaaS`
* [Statable](https://statable.com/) - Privacy-first, cookieless web analytics hosted in the EU. Import your Google Analytics 4 history and keep a unified timeline. `©` `SaaS` `EU`
* [Measure.events](https://measure.events) - Privacy-first web analytics with an agent-native API, MCP server, and natural language summaries. Built for AI-forward teams. `©` `SaaS`
* [d8a.tech](https://d8a.tech/) - Open-source, GA4/Matomo-compatible analytics. Warehouse-native storage in BigQuery, ClickHouse, or files (CSV/Parquet). Available as EU-hosted cloud or self-hosted. `MIT` `SaaS` `Self-Hosted` `Go/Docker`

##### APIs, Data, and ML

* [cors.dev](https://cors.dev/) - CORS proxy for calling third-party APIs from frontend code: prefix any HTTPS URL with `https://proxy.cors.dev/`. Free without signup or an API key for GET and HEAD requests, 600 requests per minute per IP, responses up to 1 MiB, no monthly quota.
* [Dynamic Document API](https://dynamicdocumentapi.com/) - Turn data into pixel-perfect PDFs and images. The free plan includes 50 renders per month and access to 2 templates from the template gallery or your own.

##### Tools for Teams and Collaboration

* [MergeMe](https://mergeme.dev/) - MergeMe brings GitHub/GitLab PRs/MRs directly into Slack as 1 constantly updating card. It adds CI status, mirrored comments, @mentions, reminders & more. Free for teams up to 5 developers & 1 channel mapping.

##### Monitoring

* [OpenUptime](https://openuptime.app) - Hosted uptime monitoring and status pages. Free tier includes 3 active monitors with 5-minute check intervals, 26,000 checks and 100 emails per month, and 30 days of check history.

##### Email

* [DMARCmetric](https://dmarcmetric.com/) - DMARC report monitoring with named sending sources, SPF/DKIM alignment and plain-English summaries. Free for 1 domain: 10,000 messages/month, 30-day history, email alerts, no credit card.

##### Data Visualization on Maps

* [opengeocoding.com](https://opengeocoding.com) - Source-available geocoder in Rust: address search, autocomplete and reverse geocoding on open data. Self-host it or use the hosted API. Fifty thousand free queries/month.

#### [12. Awesome Zig](/content/zigcc/awesome-zig/README.md)

##### Fundamentals / Documentation and Testing

* [newmathphys/kern (⭐8)](https://github.com/newmathphys/kern) ⭐ 9 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-21 - Zero-trust verification gateway: a claim is admitted only with machine evidence, so every Z3 proof DAG is re-checked inside KERN's own Zig kernel and each counterexample is replayed against the real function in Python (for C/Zig/Rust the counterexample comes from the SMT model with machine-type bounds). Contract annotations (`@kern:requires`/`@kern:ensures`) are checked in Python, Zig, C and Rust, and the toolchain ships 125 Lean 4 theorems with 0 `sorry`. AI-assisted.

* [guanchzhou/zig-telegram (⭐0)](https://github.com/guanchzhou/zig-telegram) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Zig | 📅 2026-10-20 - Telegram Bot API 10.3 client, pure-Zig MTProto 2.0 core, and TDLib bridge for Zig 0.17. AI-assisted.

##### Other / [Other](#other-1)

* [htmlbeautifier (⭐373)](https://github.com/threedaymonk/htmlbeautifier) ⚠️ Archived — A normaliser/beautifier for HTML that also understands embedded Ruby. Ideal for tidying up Rails templates.
* **mdformat** :warning: — CommonMark compliant Markdown formatter

##### Programming Languages / [Other](#other-1)

* [Prusti](https://www.pm.inf.ethz.ch/research/prusti.html) — A static verifier for Rust, based on the Viper verification infrastructure. By default Prusti verifies absence of panics by proving that statements such as unreachable!() and panic!() are unreachable.

#### [16. Awesome Testing](/content/TheJambo/awesome-testing/README.md)

##### Software / UI & End-to-End Testing

* [Device Hub Pro (⭐40)](https://github.com/gitemre/device-hub-pro) ⭐ 127 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-10-22 - Free, open-source macOS app for manual testing on Android emulators, Android phones and iOS simulators side by side, with live mirroring, device controls (network, location, permissions, deep links, push), logs, screenshots and screen recordings.
* [vibe-testing (⭐17)](https://github.com/AishwaryShrivastav/vibe-testing) ⭐ 17 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-15 - Open-source MCP server and CLI that scans web app routes and forms, runs browser checks with Playwright, and produces HTML QA reports.
* [App Crash Lab (⭐3)](https://github.com/aichance/business-ai-recipes/tree/main/tools/app-crash-lab) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 20 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-22 - MIT-licensed Node 22+/Playwright CLI that checks saved web-app values after reload and rejected updates, exporting state diffs and standalone Playwright tests.
* [Proxy QA Browser (⭐1)](https://github.com/UbaidBinWaris/proxy_browser) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-22 - Free, open-source desktop app to test your own web forms by US location, emulated device and browser, with recorded scenarios, consent and accessibility checks, and a CI runner.
* [Device Changer](https://deviceschanger.org) - Applies device models, build fingerprints and identifiers to rooted Android test devices to reproduce device-specific bugs.

##### Software / Accessibility & Usability Testing

* [Oh My Android (⭐60)](https://github.com/ateymoori/oh-my-android) ⭐ 69 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-10-22 - Free, open-source macOS app for checking Android apps on emulators and phones in dark mode, large font sizes, RTL and TalkBack, with a TalkBack-order accessibility audit.
* [AuditReady](https://feaefe371424d0725b266468856c9101.ctonew.app/) - Runs 12 deterministic HTML checks on a page against WCAG 2.2 and returns the matching criterion and a suggested fix for each finding, then turns them into a printable report an agency can hand to its client. Free instant scan; the full report is a one-off $29.

##### Software / API Testing

* [SolidPing (⭐17)](https://github.com/fclairamb/solidping) ⭐ 25 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-10-22 - Open-source (AGPL-3.0) uptime and API monitoring with a free hosted tier. Runs HTTP, gRPC, WebSocket and custom JS checks on a schedule and alerts when an endpoint fails.
* [Openhook (⭐1)](https://github.com/openhook-dev/openhook-inbox) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-19 - Open-source webhook inbox for capturing callbacks and inspecting request headers and bodies through a browser, REST API, or MCP.
* [API Spector](https://api-spector.dev) - Open-source API clien, mock server and contract testing tool. Zero telemetry.

##### Software / AI & LLM Testing

* [fanout-check (⭐1)](https://github.com/sunxiuguo/fanout-check) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-21 - Offline Python library and CLI that checks observed concurrency, overlap, and launch timing in explicitly grouped tool or agent calls.

##### Software / Service Virtualization

* [MockForge (⭐12)](https://github.com/SaaSy-Solutions/mockforge) ⭐ 12 | 🐛 89 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-10-22 - Open-source API mock server written in Rust for HTTP/OpenAPI, gRPC, GraphQL, WebSocket, Kafka, MQTT and AMQP, with data generation and latency/fault injection.
* [fieldtwin](https://nickgeorgeai.com/fieldtwin) - Open-source, local, stateful twins of the Jobber and QuickBooks Online APIs for integration and AI agent tests, with checkpoints, rewind, and faults on demand.

##### Software / Visual Testing

* [CurlShot](https://curlshot.com) - Screenshot API for capturing baseline and comparison images of web pages in CI as PNG, JPEG, WebP or PDF, with full-page and CSS-selector element capture, device presets and ad/cookie-banner blocking. Free plan available.

##### Software / Test Data Management

* [TempMail Hub](https://tempmailhub.net/) - Free disposable inbox for manual or scripted signup and email-verification testing, with a selectable lifetime from 10 minutes to 7 days and no account required.
* [ByteRivet Sample Files](https://byterivet.com/test-files/) - Free browser-based generator for exact-size dummy files from 1 KB to 2 GB in 22 formats (PDF, PNG, JPG, DOCX, XLSX, CSV, JSON, ZIP, MP4, WAV and more) for upload-limit and file-handling tests. Files are built locally, no upload or signup.
* [FormFiller test data tools](https://formfillerapp.com/tools/) - Free browser-based generators for fake addresses, identities, IBANs, test card numbers and bulk CSV/JSON/SQL data in 22
  locales.

##### Software / Browser Extensions & Utilities

* [Keploy API Test Recorder](https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/keploy-api-test-recorder/ohcclfkaidblnjnggclkiecgkpgldihe) - Chrome extension that records API calls from your browser session and turns them into API tests.

##### Software / Performance & Load Testing

* [vhr-bench (⭐2)](https://github.com/vpshostreview/vhr-vps-benchmark) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Shell | 📅 2026-10-16 - One-minute Linux server benchmark of CPU, memory and 4k random disk IOPS using sysbench and fio.

##### Software / Test Automation Frameworks

* [behave (⭐2)](https://github.com/emirmrkaljevic/behave) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-10-21 - Records how a command-line tool behaves (flags, stdin, exit codes, edge cases) as golden tests and replays them against another implementation, e.g. a Rust or Go rewrite.

##### Software / Static Analysis

* [SmartBench (⭐2)](https://github.com/xianyu-sheng/SmartBench) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 20 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-22 - Open-source CLI for evidence-constrained code audits: deterministic static analysis owns the facts and LLM hypotheses must be validated against the code before they become findings.

##### Newsletters / Static Analysis

* [RoleGig QA & Testing](https://rolegig.com/qa-testing/) - Weekly list of QA, test automation and SDET jobs in India, the US, Canada, Europe, Australia and the Gulf.
